-- Book of the Depths | CM1 Linoria Edition
-- Contains the original 161 gameplay modules with a rewritten Linoria UI integration.
-- Bundled by luabundle {"version":"1.7.0"}
local __bundle_require, __bundle_loaded, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules = (function(superRequire)
	local loadingPlaceholder = {[{}] = true}

	local register
	local modules = {}

	local require
	local loaded = {}

	register = function(name, body)
		if not modules[name] then
			modules[name] = body
		end
	end

	require = function(name)
		local loadedModule = loaded[name]

		if loadedModule then
			if loadedModule == loadingPlaceholder then
				return nil
			end
		else
			if not modules[name] then
				if not superRequire then
					local identifier = type(name) == 'string' and '\"' .. name .. '\"' or tostring(name)
					error('Tried to require ' .. identifier .. ', but no such module has been registered')
				else
					return superRequire(name)
				end
			end

			loaded[name] = loadingPlaceholder
			loadedModule = modules[name](require, loaded, register, modules)
			loaded[name] = loadedModule
		end

		return loadedModule
	end

	return require, loaded, register, modules
end)(require)
__bundle_register("__root", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
if not shared then
	return
end


local services = {
	Players = game:GetService("Players"),
	CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService"),
}

if not game:IsLoaded() then
	game.Loaded:Wait()
end

-- Was a blind `task.wait(3)`. Instead wait for the client to actually be ready -- LocalPlayer +
-- PlayerScripts (where the anti-cheat lives, so Hooking can neutralize it) --
-- capped at the same 3s ceiling so worst-case behaviour is unchanged. On a normal load these exist
-- in well under a second, so this shaves ~2s off every startup.
do
	local deadline = os.clock() + 3

	repeat
		task.wait()

		local localPlayer = services.Players.LocalPlayer

		if localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts") then
			break
		end
	until os.clock() >= deadline
end

loadstring("getfenv().LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE = function(...) return ... end")()

getfenv().PP_SCRAMBLE_NUM = function(...)
	return ...
end

getfenv().PP_SCRAMBLE_STR = function(...)
	return ...
end

getfenv().PP_SCRAMBLE_RE_NUM = function(...)
	return ...
end

---@module Utility.Profiler
local Profiler = require("Utility/Profiler")

---@module Luma
local Luma
do
	local ok, result = xpcall(function()
		return require("Luma")
	end, function(e)
		warn("[Luma/diag] require(Luma) LOAD ERROR: " .. tostring(e))
		warn("[Luma/diag] " .. debug.traceback())
		return e
	end)
	if not ok then
		return
	end
	Luma = result
end

local function initializeScript()
	if shared.Luma then
		pcall(shared.Luma.detach)

		Luma.queued = shared.Luma.queued
	end

	shared.Luma = Luma
	shared.Luma.init()
end

---@param error string
local function onInitializeError(error)
	warn("[Luma/diag] INIT ERROR: " .. tostring(error))
	warn("[Luma/diag] " .. debug.traceback())
	pcall(Luma.detach)
end

Profiler.run("Main_InitializeScript", function(...)
	return xpcall(initializeScript, onInitializeError, ...)
end)
end)
__bundle_register("Luma", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
local Luma = { queued = false, silent = false, dpscanning = false, norpc = false, verbose = false }

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.JSON
local JSON = require("Utility/JSON")

---@module Utility.TaskSpawner
local TaskSpawner = require("Utility/TaskSpawner")

---@module Utility.Environment
local Environment = require("Utility/Environment")

---@module Game.Hooking
local Hooking = require("Game/Hooking")

---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")

---@module Menu
local Menu = require("Menu")

---@module Features
local Features = require("Features")

---@module Utility.ControlModule
local ControlModule = require("Utility/ControlModule")

---@module Game.InputClient
local InputClient = require("Game/InputClient")

---@module Game.PlayerScanning
local PlayerScanning = require("Game/PlayerScanning")

---@module Game.Timings.SaveManager
local SaveManager = require("Game/Timings/SaveManager")

---@module Features.Combat.StateListener
local StateListener = require("Features/Combat/StateListener")

---@module Game.EffectDispatcher
local EffectDispatcher = require("Game/EffectDispatcher")

---@module Features.Admin

---@module Utility.PersistentData
local PersistentData = require("Utility/PersistentData")

---@module Game.KeyHandling
local KeyHandling = require("Game/KeyHandling")

---@module Game.QueuedBlocking
local QueuedBlocking = require("Game/QueuedBlocking")

---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

---@module Game.Timings.ModuleManager
local ModuleManager = require("Game/Timings/ModuleManager")

---@module Utility.CoreGuiManager
local CoreGuiManager = require("Utility/CoreGuiManager")

---@module Game.ServerHop
local ServerHop = require("Game/ServerHop")

---@module Game.Wipe
local Wipe = require("Game/Wipe")

---@module Features.Automation.EchoFarm
local EchoFarm = require("Features/Automation/EchoFarm")

---@module Features.Automation.AutoLayer2
local AutoLayer2 = require("Features/Automation/AutoLayer2")

---@module Features.Automation.Objects.General
local General = require("Features/Automation/Objects/General")

local lumaMaid = Maid.new()

local LOBBY_PLACE_ID = 4111023553
local DEPTHS_PLACE_ID = 5735553160
local CHIME_LOBBY_PLACE_ID = 12559711136

local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local playersService = game:GetService("Players")

local startTimestamp = os.clock()

---@note: Stops every farm but keeps its resume flag, so a reload restarts exactly one copy via the resume blocks in init.
local function stop_farms()
	local farms = {
		{ require("Features/Automation/EchoFarm"), "efdata" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoLayer2"), "l2data" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoTo1"), "to1data" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoVoI"), "voidata" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoFerryman"), "afdata" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoTitus"), "titusdata" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoAuthority"), "authoritydata" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoDuke"), "dukedata" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoMooneyrie"), "mooneyriedata" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoNotes"), "notesdata" },
		{ require("Features/Automation/AutoSaramed"), "sarameddata" },
	}

	for _, farm in ipairs(farms) do
		local module, key = farm[1], farm[2]
		local saved = PersistentData.get(key)

		pcall(module.stop)

		if saved ~= nil and PersistentData.get(key) ~= saved then
			pcall(PersistentData.set, key, saved)
		end
	end
end

function Luma.init()
	local localPlayer = nil

	repeat
		task.wait()
	until game:IsLoaded()

	-- Client prep -- the loading-screen A-spam (resume-gated) and the LocalPlayer/PlayerScripts/Character
	-- wait -- now runs in the loader (luarmor VM) BEFORE the core is executed, so the client is already
	-- ready here. We just resolve LocalPlayer (instant) and hook.
	repeat
		localPlayer = playersService.LocalPlayer
		task.wait()
	until localPlayer ~= nil

	if game.PlaceId ~= LOBBY_PLACE_ID then
		-- Start the init timer here. The character wait already happened in the loader, so this measures
		-- actual script loading, not the spawn wait.
		startTimestamp = os.clock()

		Hooking.earlyInit()
	end

	if isfile and isfile("smarker.txt") then
		Luma.silent = true
	end

	if isfile and isfile("dpscanning.txt") then
		Luma.dpscanning = true
	end

	if isfile and isfile("norpc.txt") then
		Luma.norpc = true
	end

	if isfile and isfile("verbose.txt") then
		Luma.verbose = true
	end


	if game.PlaceId == CHIME_LOBBY_PLACE_ID then
		return Logger.warn("Script has initialized in the Chime lobby.")
	end

	if game.PlaceId ~= LOBBY_PLACE_ID then
		KeyHandling.init()

		Hooking.init()
	end

	CoreGuiManager.set()

	PersistentData.init()

	if game.PlaceId == LOBBY_PLACE_ID then
		Logger.warn("Script has initialized in the lobby.")
	end

	if game.PlaceId == LOBBY_PLACE_ID then
		if PersistentData.get("shslot") then
			return ServerHop.lobby()
		end

		if PersistentData.get("wdata") then
			return Wipe.lobby()
		end
	end

	PersistentData.set("shslot", nil)

	if game.PlaceId == DEPTHS_PLACE_ID then
		if PersistentData.get("wdata") then
			Wipe.depths()
		end
	end

	if PersistentData.get("efdata") then
		EchoFarm.start()
	end

	if PersistentData.get("l2data") then
		AutoLayer2.start()
	end

	if game.PlaceId == LOBBY_PLACE_ID then
		return
	end

	QueuedBlocking.init()

	SaveManager.init()

	ModuleManager.refresh()

	ControlModule.init()

	Features.init()

	Menu.init()

	PlayerScanning.init()

	StateListener.init()

	-- Single shared EffectReplicator connection for every always-on consumer (StateListener, Removal, ...).
	-- Connect AFTER they've registered their hooks above so the initial replay reaches all of them.
	EffectDispatcher.init()


	-- Resume Auto Builder after a server hop (abdata survives the teleport via MemStorage). Only
	-- resumes if it was active on this same data slot; reloads the same link + target and restarts.
	if PersistentData.get("abdata") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoBuilder = require("Features/Automation/AutoBuilder")

			-- Wait for the character's DataSlot to be available before checking the slot match.
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoBuilder.currentSlot() ~= nil or os.clock() >= deadline

			local build = AutoBuilder.resume()
			if build then
				pcall(function()
					require("Menu/AutoBuilderWindow").show(build, AutoBuilder.classified)
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Resume Auto To1 after a server hop / place teleport (to1data survives via MemStorage). Wait
	-- until we're in a place it can run (Etrean Luminant or the Trial arena) before restarting.
	if PersistentData.get("to1data") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoTo1 = require("Features/Automation/AutoTo1")
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoTo1.inValidPlace() or os.clock() >= deadline

			if AutoTo1.inValidPlace() then
				AutoTo1.start()
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Resume Auto VoI after a server hop / place teleport (voidata survives via MemStorage). Wait until
	-- we're in a place it can run (the VoI place or the Shogun arena).
	if PersistentData.get("voidata") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoVoI = require("Features/Automation/AutoVoI")
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoVoI.inValidPlace() or os.clock() >= deadline

			if AutoVoI.inValidPlace() then
				AutoVoI.start()
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Resume Auto Ferryman after a server hop (afdata survives the teleport via MemStorage). Wait
	-- until we're actually in the Eastern Luminant (ValleyExit may still be streaming in) before
	-- restarting the farm.
	if PersistentData.get("afdata") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoFerryman = require("Features/Automation/AutoFerryman")
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoFerryman.inEasternLuminant() or AutoFerryman.inBossDungeon() or os.clock() >= deadline

			if AutoFerryman.inEasternLuminant() or AutoFerryman.inBossDungeon() then
				AutoFerryman.start()
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Resume Auto Titus after a server hop / merit<->dungeon teleport (titusdata survives via MemStorage).
	-- Wait until we're back in the merit hub or the Titus dungeon before restarting.
	if PersistentData.get("titusdata") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoTitus = require("Features/Automation/AutoTitus")
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoTitus.inValidPlace() or os.clock() >= deadline

			if AutoTitus.inValidPlace() then
				AutoTitus.start()
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Resume Auto Authority after a server hop (authoritydata survives via MemStorage). Wait until we're
	-- back in the Eastern Luminant before restarting.
	if PersistentData.get("authoritydata") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoAuthority = require("Features/Automation/AutoAuthority")
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoAuthority.inValidPlace() or os.clock() >= deadline

			if AutoAuthority.inValidPlace() then
				AutoAuthority.start()
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Resume Auto Duke after a server hop / dungeon teleport (dukedata survives via MemStorage).
	if PersistentData.get("dukedata") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoDuke = require("Features/Automation/AutoDuke")
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoDuke.inValidPlace() or (localPlayer and localPlayer:GetAttribute("Dungeon")) or os.clock() >= deadline

			AutoDuke.start()
		end)
	end

	-- Resume Auto Mooneyrie after a server hop (mooneyriedata survives via MemStorage).
	if PersistentData.get("mooneyriedata") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoMooneyrie = require("Features/Automation/AutoMooneyrie")
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoMooneyrie.inValidPlace() or os.clock() >= deadline

			if AutoMooneyrie.inValidPlace() then
				AutoMooneyrie.start()
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Resume Auto Notes after a server hop / wipe (notesdata survives via MemStorage).
	if PersistentData.get("notesdata") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoNotes = require("Features/Automation/AutoNotes")
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoNotes.inValidPlace() or os.clock() >= deadline

			if AutoNotes.inValidPlace() then
				AutoNotes.start()
			end
		end)
	end

	-- Resume Auto Saramed after a server hop / dungeon teleport (sarameddata survives via MemStorage).
	if PersistentData.get("sarameddata") then
		task.spawn(function()
			local AutoSaramed = require("Features/Automation/AutoSaramed")
			local deadline = os.clock() + 60
			repeat
				task.wait(1)
			until AutoSaramed.inValidPlace() or os.clock() >= deadline

			if AutoSaramed.inValidPlace() then
				AutoSaramed.start()
			end
		end)
	end

	if not Luma.silent then
		Logger.notify("Script has been initialized in %ims.", (os.clock() - startTimestamp) * 1000)
	end

	if not PersistentData.get("fli") then
		PersistentData.set("fli", os.time())
	end

	-- No rich presence or session telemetry is broadcast.

end

function Luma.detach()
	pcall(lumaMaid.clean, lumaMaid)

	pcall(stop_farms)

	pcall(ModuleManager.detach)

	pcall(Menu.detach)

	pcall(QueuedBlocking.detach)

	pcall(ControlModule.detach)

	pcall(InputClient.detach)

	pcall(Features.detach)

	pcall(SaveManager.detach)

	pcall(PlayerScanning.detach)

	pcall(CoreGuiManager.clear)

	pcall(StateListener.detach)

	pcall(EffectDispatcher.detach)

	local bloxstrapRPCModule = type(get_bloxstrap_rpc) == "function" and get_bloxstrap_rpc() or nil

	if bloxstrapRPCModule then
		pcall(bloxstrapRPCModule.SetRichPresence, {
			details = "Luma (Detached)",
			state = Environment.isTester() and "Detached from script - something broke, fixing a bug, or a hot-reload."
				or "Detached from script - something broke or a hot-reload.",
			timeStart = PersistentData.get("fli") or os.time(),
			largeImage = {
				assetId = Environment.isTester() and 11289930484 or 109802578297970,
				hoverText = Environment.isTester() and "Developing Deepwoken" or "Not Using Deepwoken",
			},
			smallImage = {
				assetId = Environment.isTester() and 15828456271 or 17278571027,
				hoverText = Environment.isTester() and "Developing Deepwoken" or "Not Using Deepwoken",
			},
		})
	end

	pcall(Hooking.detach)

	Logger.warn("Script has been detached.")
end

return Luma
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Automation/AutoSaramed", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Auto Saramed: a luma port of project-rain-oss's `auto_saramed.lua`, on the AutomationStruct +
-- StateMachine framework. Enters the Saramed deepdrill dungeon (via Malisae in the Eastern Luminant),
-- clears the mobs, mines the magma ore, deposits it into the drill, loots, then resets the dungeon and
-- repeats. Keeps hunger/thirst topped up (gather + craft bread, drink at the well).
--
-- Public surface:
--   AutoSaramed.start() / .stop() / .inValidPlace()  (Eastern Luminant or the Saramed dungeon)
-- Persistence key: "sarameddata" { slot, startedAt }.
local AutoSaramed = {}

---@module Utility.PersistentData
local PersistentData = require("Utility/PersistentData")
---@module Utility.Prompt
local Prompt = require("Utility/Prompt")
---@module Game.ServerHop
local ServerHop = require("Game/ServerHop")
---@module Features.Game.Tweening
local Tweening = require("Features/Game/Tweening")
---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")
---@module GUI.Library
local Library = require("GUI/Library")
---@module Utility.CoreGuiManager
local CoreGuiManager = require("Utility/CoreGuiManager")
---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")
---@module Game.AntiAFK
local AntiAFK = require("Game/AntiAFK")
---@module Features.Automation.Objects.StateMachine
local StateMachine = require("Features/Automation/Objects/StateMachine")
---@module Features.Automation.Objects.AutomationStruct
local AutomationStruct = require("Features/Automation/Objects/AutomationStruct")
---@module Features.Automation.Objects.General
local General = require("Features/Automation/Objects/General")
---@module Features.Automation.Objects.Teleports
local Teleports = require("Features/Automation/Objects/Teleports")

local players = game:GetService("Players")
local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local SARAMED_PLACE_ID = 8668476218 -- the deepdrill (Saramed) dungeon.
local EASTERN_PLACE_ID = 6473861193 -- Eastern Luminant (Malisae, the dungeon entrance).
local MAIN_GAME_PLACE_ID = 6032399813 -- Etrean overworld (route back to Eastern from here).
local DEPTHS_PLACE_ID = 5735553160 -- The Depths (should never be here; stop if we are).

local MOVE_ID = "AutoSaramed_Move"
local MIN_HEALTH = 0.5 -- retreat/bail on the fight at or below this health fraction (raised from PR's 0.25 -- retreat earlier, at half HP).
local ATTACH_DISTANCE = 8 -- studs above the mob's torso when attaching (PR default: 8).

local running = false
local struct = nil
local maid = Maid.new()
local screenGui, elapsedLabel = nil, nil
local autoDepositThread = nil
local disconnectHandlerSet = false
local previousAutoDefense = nil -- EnableAutoDefense state before the run, restored on stop

local function isSaramed()
	return game.PlaceId == SARAMED_PLACE_ID
end

-- =========================================================================================================
-- Accessors.
-- =========================================================================================================
local function localPlayer()
	return players.LocalPlayer
end
local function character()
	local lp = localPlayer()
	return lp and lp.Character
end
local function root()
	local c = character()
	return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local function humanoid()
	local c = character()
	return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end
local function backpack()
	local lp = localPlayer()
	return lp and lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
end
local function playerGui()
	local lp = localPlayer()
	return lp and lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
end
local function currentSlot()
	local lp = localPlayer()
	return lp and lp:GetAttribute("DataSlot")
end
local function isRunning()
	return running
end
function AutoSaramed.inValidPlace()
	return game.PlaceId == SARAMED_PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId == EASTERN_PLACE_ID or game.PlaceId == MAIN_GAME_PLACE_ID
end
local function getEffectReplicator()
	local ok, mod = pcall(function()
		return require(replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("EffectReplicator"))
	end)
	return ok and mod or nil
end

-- =========================================================================================================
-- Overlay.
-- =========================================================================================================
local function guiParent()
	return (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
end
local function mk(class, props, parent)
	local instance = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props) do
		instance[k] = v
	end
	if parent then
		instance.Parent = parent
	end
	return instance
end
local function buildOverlay()
	if screenGui then
		return
	end
	screenGui = CoreGuiManager.imark(Instance.new("ScreenGui"))
	screenGui.Name = "LumaAutoSaramedOverlay"
	screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
	screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
	screenGui.DisplayOrder = 9999
	local protectGui = protectgui or (syn and syn.protect_gui) or function() end
	pcall(protectGui, screenGui)
	screenGui.Parent = guiParent()
	local outer = mk("Frame", {
		Name = "Outer",
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 46),
		Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 78),
		BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
		BorderColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 1,
		ZIndex = 200,
	}, screenGui)
	Library:AddToRegistry(outer, { BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor", BorderColor3 = "OutlineColor" })
	local title = mk("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 6),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 20),
		FontFace = Library.Font,
		TextSize = 16,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
		Text = "Auto Saramed",
		ZIndex = 201,
	}, outer)
	Library:AddToRegistry(title, { TextColor3 = "AccentColor" })
	elapsedLabel = mk("TextLabel", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 30),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 16),
		FontFace = Library.Font,
		TextSize = 14,
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Text = "Elapsed: 0s",
		ZIndex = 201,
	}, outer)
	Library:AddToRegistry(elapsedLabel, { TextColor3 = "FontColor" })
	local stopButton = mk("TextButton", {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 1, -30),
		Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 22),
		AutoButtonColor = true,
		BackgroundColor3 = Library:GetDarkerColor(Library.MainColor),
		BorderColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
		BorderSizePixel = 1,
		FontFace = Library.Font,
		TextSize = 15,
		TextColor3 = Library.FontColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
		Text = "Stop",
		ZIndex = 201,
	}, outer)
	Library:AddToRegistry(stopButton, {
		BackgroundColor3 = function()
			return Library:GetDarkerColor(Library.MainColor)
		end,
		BorderColor3 = "OutlineColor",
		TextColor3 = "FontColor",
	})
	stopButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		AutoSaramed.stop()
	end)
	pcall(function()
		Library:MakeDraggable(outer)
	end)
end
local function destroyOverlay()
	if screenGui then
		pcall(function()
			screenGui:Destroy()
		end)
		screenGui = nil
	end
	elapsedLabel = nil
end
-- Stage text was removed; kept as a no-op so the many callers don't need changing.
local function setStage(_) end

local function formatDuration(seconds)
	seconds = math.floor(seconds)
	if seconds < 60 then
		return seconds .. "s"
	end
	local minutes = math.floor(seconds / 60)
	if seconds < 3600 then
		return minutes .. "m " .. (seconds % 60) .. "s"
	end
	return math.floor(minutes / 60) .. "h " .. (minutes % 60) .. "m"
end

local function runElapsed()
	while running do
		local data = PersistentData.get("sarameddata")
		if elapsedLabel and data then
			elapsedLabel.Text = "Elapsed: " .. formatDuration(math.max(0, os.time() - (data.startedAt or os.time())))
		end
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
end

-- =========================================================================================================
-- Movement + helpers.
-- =========================================================================================================
local function setSpeed(value)
	value = (Options and Options.SaramedTweenSpeed and Options.SaramedTweenSpeed.Value) or 100
	if Options and Options.TweenStudsPerSecond and Options.TweenStudsPerSecond.Value ~= value then
		Options.TweenStudsPerSecond:SetValue(value)
	end
end
local function tempTween(cframe, speed)
	setSpeed(speed or 170)
	Tweening.goal(MOVE_ID, cframe, false)
	local goalPos = cframe.Position
	while running do
		local r = root()
		if r and (r.Position - goalPos).Magnitude <= 4 then
			break
		end
		task.wait()
	end
	Tweening.stop(MOVE_ID)
end
local function tweenGoal(cframe, speed)
	setSpeed(speed or 100)
	Tweening.goal(MOVE_ID, cframe, false)
end
local function playerCheck(mag, custom)
	local origin = custom or (root() and root().Position)
	if not origin then
		return false
	end
	for _, player in next, players:GetPlayers() do
		if player ~= localPlayer() and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			if (origin - player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= mag then
				return true
			end
		end
	end
	return false
end
local function serverHop()
	pcall(function()
		ServerHop.hop(currentSlot(), true)
	end)
end
local function playerSafeTween(cf, speed)
	if playerCheck(200) then
		serverHop()
		while task.wait() do
		end
		return
	end
	local r = root()
	if not r then
		return
	end
	local y = math.random(10000, 30000)
	r.CFrame = r.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, y, 0)
	tempTween(cf + Vector3.new(0, y, 0), speed)
	if playerCheck(200, cf.Position) then
		serverHop()
		while task.wait() do
		end
		return
	end
	r = root()
	if r then
		r.CFrame = cf
	end
	task.wait(0.3)
end
local function readyWeapon()
	pcall(function()
		character().CharacterHandler.Requests.DrawWeapon:FireServer(true)
	end)
end
local function m1Hold(state)
	local feat = struct and struct.active_features and struct.active_features["m1_hold"]
	if feat then
		feat.held = state
	end
end
local function healthCheck(percent)
	local hum = humanoid()
	return hum ~= nil and hum.Health <= (hum.MaxHealth * percent)
end
local function statValue(name)
	local c = character()
	return c and c:FindFirstChild(name)
end
local function clampCFrame(cf)
	return CFrame.new(Vector3.new(cf.Position.X, math.clamp(cf.Position.Y, 0, 1000), cf.Position.Z))
end

-- (Food/water refill removed -- hunger and thirst upkeep both dropped.)

-- =========================================================================================================
-- Combat + mining.
-- =========================================================================================================
local function foundMobs()
	for _, mob in next, workspace:WaitForChild("Live"):GetChildren() do
		if mob:GetAttribute("MOB_rich_name") then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function closestMob()
	local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
	if not live then
		return nil
	end
	local closest, shortest = nil, math.huge
	for _, model in next, live:GetChildren() do
		if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= character() and model.Name:sub(1, 1) == "." then
			local r = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model.PrimaryPart
			local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if r and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
				local torso = model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
				if not (torso and torso:FindFirstChild("RagdollAttach")) then
					local dist = (r.Position - (root() and root().Position or Vector3.zero)).Magnitude
					if dist < shortest then
						shortest = dist
						closest = model
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
	return closest
end

local function killTarget(target)
	if not target or not target:IsA("Model") then
		return
	end
	if target.Name:match(localPlayer().Name) then
		return
	end

	readyWeapon()
	if target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
		tempTween(target.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame, 170)
	end

	local attach = task.spawn(function()
		local activeTween = false
		while running do
			if not target.Parent then
					break
				end
				local hrp = target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not hrp then
					task.wait()
					continue
				end
				local cf
			if target.Name:lower():find("buncle") or target.Name:lower():find("knight") then
				cf = clampCFrame(hrp.CFrame) * CFrame.new(0, -ATTACH_DISTANCE, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
			else
				cf = clampCFrame(hrp.CFrame) * CFrame.new(0, ATTACH_DISTANCE, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
			end
			local r = root()
			if r then
				r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
				if (r.Position - cf.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
					tweenGoal(cf, 100)
					activeTween = true
				else
					if activeTween then
						Tweening.stop(MOVE_ID)
						activeTween = false
					end
					r.CFrame = cf
				end
			end
			task.wait()
		end
		Tweening.stop(MOVE_ID)
	end)

	m1Hold(true)
	local startedAt = tick()
	repeat
		task.wait()
		m1Hold(true)
		local hum = target:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	until healthCheck(MIN_HEALTH) or target.Parent == nil or (hum and hum.Health <= 0) or (tick() - startedAt > 20) or not running
	pcall(function()
		task.cancel(attach)
	end)
	m1Hold(false)
	Tweening.stop(MOVE_ID)
	return healthCheck(MIN_HEALTH)
end

local function closestOre()
	local closest
	for _, ore in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
		if ore:IsA("MeshPart") and ore.Name:match("MagmaOre") and ore:FindFirstChild("InteractPrompt") then
			if not closest then
				closest = ore
			elseif (root().Position - ore.Position).Magnitude < (root().Position - closest.Position).Magnitude then
				closest = ore
			end
		end
	end
	return closest
end

local function mineOre(target)
	if not target or target.Parent == nil or not target:IsA("MeshPart") or not target.Name:match("MagmaOre") then
		return false
	end
	tempTween(target.CFrame, 170)
	local r = root()
	if r then
		r.CFrame = target.CFrame
	end
	xpcall(function()
		for _ = 1, 60 do
			if not running then
				break
			end
			if root() then
				root().CFrame = target.CFrame
			end
			pcall(function()
				local prompt = target:FindFirstChild("InteractPrompt")
				if prompt then
					Prompt.interact(prompt)
				end
			end)
			task.wait()
		end
		local debounce = tick()
		repeat
			task.wait()
		until not target:FindFirstChild("InteractPrompt") or tick() - debounce > 5 or not running
	end, function() end)
	return true
end

local function depositOre()
	local deepdrill = workspace:FindFirstChild("Deepdrill")
	local dungeondrill = deepdrill and deepdrill:FindFirstChild("DungeonDrill")
	local fuelport = dungeondrill and dungeondrill:FindFirstChild("FuelPort")
	local hit = fuelport and fuelport:FindFirstChild("Hit")
	if hit then
		tempTween(hit.CFrame, 170)
		task.wait(1)
	end
end

local function grabChest()
	local thrown = workspace:FindFirstChild("Thrown")
	if not thrown then
		return
	end
	for _, chest in next, thrown:GetChildren() do
		if chest:FindFirstChild("Lid") then
			tempTween(chest.Lid.CFrame, 170)
			local t = tick()
			repeat
				task.wait(0.1)
				pcall(function()
					Prompt.interact(chest:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt"))
				end)
				pcall(function()
					tempTween(chest.Lid.CFrame, 170)
				end)
			until (playerGui() and playerGui():FindFirstChild("ChoicePrompt")) or not running or tick() - t > 5
			local t2 = tick()
			repeat
				task.wait(0.1)
			until not (playerGui() and playerGui():FindFirstChild("ChoicePrompt")) or not running or tick() - t2 > 5
			break
		end
	end
end

-- =========================================================================================================
-- Stages.
-- =========================================================================================================
local function pathToSaramed()
	if isSaramed() then
		return
	end
	if game.PlaceId == EASTERN_PLACE_ID then
		setStage("entering Saramed")
		local malisaeCF = CFrame.new(-2835.7998046875, 136.5481414794922, 3442.84814453125, 0.541101158, 0, -0.840957522, 0, 1, 0, 0.840957522, 0, 0.541101158)
		playerSafeTween(malisaeCF, 170)
		task.wait(0.5)
		repeat
			task.wait()
		until workspace.NPCs:FindFirstChild("Malisae") or not running
		pcall(function()
			Prompt.interact(workspace.NPCs.Malisae.InteractPrompt)
		end)
		task.wait(3)
		pcall(function()
			replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Requests"):WaitForChild("SendDialogue"):FireServer({ choice = "Enter alone." })
		end)
		task.wait(5)
	end
end

local function checkPlayerStatus()
	-- Food/water upkeep removed; nothing to do here now. Kept as a no-op so the _status state's call to it
	-- stays valid.
end

local function startDungeon()
	if not isSaramed() then
		return
	end
	repeat
		task.wait()
	until character() or not running

	if not (backpack() and backpack():FindFirstChild("Pickaxe")) and not (character() and character():FindFirstChild("Pickaxe")) then
		Logger.notify("[Auto Saramed] Please buy a pickaxe.")
		repeat
			task.wait()
		until (backpack() and backpack():FindFirstChild("Pickaxe"))
			or (character() and character():FindFirstChild("Pickaxe"))
			or not running
	end

	local deepdrill = workspace:FindFirstChild("Deepdrill")
	if not deepdrill then
		return
	end
	local drillSwitch = deepdrill.DungeonDrill.Switch.Case
	local evatorSwitch = deepdrill.Drillevator.Switch.Case
	local drillDist = (drillSwitch.Position - (root() and root().Position or Vector3.zero)).Magnitude
	local evatorDist = (evatorSwitch.Position - (root() and root().Position or Vector3.zero)).Magnitude

	setStage("starting dungeon")
	-- Use the nearer switch (DungeonDrill vs Drillevator).
	local useDrill = drillDist < evatorDist
	local switchCase = useDrill and drillSwitch or evatorSwitch
	local switchObj = useDrill and deepdrill.DungeonDrill.Switch or deepdrill.Drillevator.Switch
	-- Keep pressing it until the drill actually starts (mobs spawn). A single press sometimes misses -- the
	-- tween hasn't landed or the prompt is briefly out of range -- and nothing retried, so completeDungeon
	-- then waited forever on mobs that never came and the farm just stopped. Re-tween + re-press each pass.
	repeat
		if root() then
			root().CFrame = CFrame.new(root().CFrame.X, switchCase.CFrame.Y, root().CFrame.Z)
		end
		-- Nudge the stand spot 2 studs right (+X) and 1 stud forward (-Z, the lever's LookVector) relative
		-- to the lever, so we land on the interactable side instead of on top of the Case.
		tempTween(switchCase.CFrame * CFrame.new(2, 0, -1), 170)
		task.wait(1)
		pcall(function()
			Prompt.interact(switchObj.InteractPrompt)
		end)
		task.wait(2)
	until foundMobs() or not running
end

local function completeDungeon()
	if not isSaramed() then
		return
	end
	setStage("clearing mobs")
	repeat
		task.wait()
	until foundMobs() or not running

	repeat
		pcall(function()
			killTarget(closestMob())
		end)
		if healthCheck(MIN_HEALTH) or not running then
			break
		end
		task.wait()
	until not foundMobs()

	if not running or healthCheck(MIN_HEALTH) then
		return
	end

	setStage("mining ore")
	for _ = 0, 5 do
		local ore
		repeat
			ore = closestOre()
			task.wait()
		until ore ~= nil or not running
		if not running then
			break
		end
		mineOre(ore)
	end
	if not running then
		return
	end

	depositOre()

	-- Keep mining until the fuel bar is full (green).
	local function fuelFull()
		local ok, full = pcall(function()
			local bar = workspace.Deepdrill.DungeonDrill.FuelPort.Display.SurfaceGui.Bar
			return bar.BackgroundColor3:ToHex() == Color3.fromRGB(141, 239, 112):ToHex()
		end)
		return ok and full
	end
	if not fuelFull() then
		repeat
			for _, ore in next, workspace:GetDescendants() do
				if ore:IsA("MeshPart") and ore.Name:match("MagmaOre") then
					if not running then
						break
					end
					mineOre(ore)
					break
				end
			end
			depositOre()
			task.wait()
		until fuelFull() or not running
	end

	if not running then
		return
	end
	setStage("looting")
	grabChest()
end

local function resetDungeon()
	if not isSaramed() or not running then
		return
	end
	setStage("resetting dungeon")
	local deepdrill = workspace:FindFirstChild("Deepdrill")
	local dungeondrill = deepdrill and deepdrill:FindFirstChild("DungeonDrill")
	local radio = dungeondrill and dungeondrill:FindFirstChild("Radio")
	local destructibles = workspace:FindFirstChild("Destructibles")
	if not radio or not destructibles then
		return
	end

	-- Retreat uses the radio (workspace.Deepdrill.DungeonDrill.Radio), NOT the lever -- the lever is for
	-- going deeper, the radio is for retreating. Tween onto the RADIO itself (GetPivot handles part-or-model)
	-- and press E on it (same as every other interaction) until the retreat ChoicePrompt shows, then confirm.
	tempTween(radio:GetPivot(), 170)
	local vim = Instance.new("VirtualInputManager")
	repeat
		task.wait()
		pcall(function()
			vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
			task.wait(0.1)
			vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
		end)
		tempTween(radio:GetPivot(), 170)
	until (playerGui() and playerGui():FindFirstChild("ChoicePrompt")) or not running
	vim:Destroy()
	pcall(function()
		playerGui().ChoicePrompt.Choice:FireServer(true)
	end)

	local campfire = destructibles:FindFirstChild("Campfire")
	if not campfire then
		return
	end
	local dist = math.huge
	repeat
		task.wait()
		local part = campfire:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Part")
		if part then
			dist = (part.Position - (root() and root().Position or Vector3.zero)).Magnitude
		end
	until dist <= 150 or not running

	if not healthCheck(0.7) then
		return
	end

	local er = getEffectReplicator()
	repeat
		tempTween(campfire.Part.CFrame, 170)
		if root() then
			root().CFrame = campfire.Part.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)
		end
		task.wait(1)
		pcall(function()
			Prompt.interact(campfire.InteractPrompt)
		end)
		task.wait(1)
	until (er and er:FindEffect("Resting")) or not running
	repeat
		task.wait()
	until (humanoid() and humanoid().Health >= humanoid().MaxHealth * 0.7) or not running
	local vim = Instance.new("VirtualInputManager")
	pcall(function()
		vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
		task.wait(0.1)
		vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
	end)
	task.wait(1)
	vim:Destroy()
end

-- On a Roblox disconnect (kick), keep the slot in MemStorage and server-hop to reconnect (PR resilience).
local function setupDisconnectHandler()
	if disconnectHandlerSet then
		return
	end
	disconnectHandlerSet = true
	local guiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
	maid:mark(guiService.ErrorMessageChanged:Connect(function()
		local ok, code = pcall(function()
			return guiService:GetErrorCode().Value
		end)
		if ok and code and code >= Enum.ConnectionError.DisconnectErrors.Value then
			pcall(function()
				local memStorage = game:GetService("MemStorageService")
				memStorage:SetItem("DataSlot", localPlayer():GetAttribute("DataSlot"))
			end)
			while task.wait() do
				pcall(serverHop)
			end
		end
	end))
end

-- Auto-deposit any MagmaOre we're carrying (runs the whole time we're in the dungeon).
local function startAutoDeposit()
	if autoDepositThread then
		return
	end
	autoDepositThread = task.spawn(function()
		while running do
			if isSaramed() and character() and character():FindFirstChild("MagmaOre") then
				pcall(function()
					local hit = workspace.Deepdrill.DungeonDrill.FuelPort.Hit
					firetouchinterest(character().MagmaOre, hit, 0)
					firetouchinterest(character().MagmaOre, hit, 1)
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.2)
		end
	end)
	maid:mark(autoDepositThread)
end

-- =========================================================================================================
-- State machine.
-- =========================================================================================================
local stateMachine = StateMachine.create({
	initial = "idle",
	events = {
		{ name = "start", from = "idle", to = "_check_area" },
		{ name = "path", from = "_check_area", to = "_path" },
		{ name = "status", from = "_path", to = "_status" },
		{ name = "dungeonStart", from = "_status", to = "_start_dungeon" },
		{ name = "dungeonComplete", from = "_start_dungeon", to = "_complete_dungeon" },
		{ name = "dungeonContinuation", from = "_complete_dungeon", to = "_start_dungeon" },
		{ name = "dungeonReset", from = "_complete_dungeon", to = "_reset_dungeon" },
		{ name = "fullReset", from = "_reset_dungeon", to = "_check_area" },
	},
	callbacks = {
		onenter_check_area = function(self)
			if not running then
				return
			end
			setStage("check area")
			setupDisconnectHandler()
			startAutoDeposit()

			-- Somehow ended up in the Depths -> stop (PR kicks here).
			if game.PlaceId == DEPTHS_PLACE_ID then
				Logger.notify("Auto Saramed: ended up in the Depths -- stopping.")
				return AutoSaramed.stop()
			end

			-- Etrean overworld -> route to Eastern.
			if game.PlaceId == MAIN_GAME_PLACE_ID then
				Teleports.eastern()
				return
			end

			self:path()
		end,
		onenter_path = function(self)
			if not running then
				return
			end
			pathToSaramed()
			self:status()
		end,
		onenter_status = function(self)
			if not running then
				return
			end
			checkPlayerStatus()
			self:dungeonStart()
		end,
		onenter_start_dungeon = function(self)
			if not running then
				return
			end
			startDungeon()
			self:dungeonComplete()
		end,
		onenter_complete_dungeon = function(self)
			if not running then
				return
			end
			completeDungeon()

			-- Keep diving unless we're low or hit the bottom (2.00 depth).
			local atBottom = false
			pcall(function()
				atBottom = workspace.Deepdrill.DungeonDrill.DepthDisplay.SurfaceGui.TextLabel.Text:find("2.00") ~= nil
			end)
			if not healthCheck(MIN_HEALTH) and not atBottom then
				task.defer(function()
					self:dungeonContinuation()
				end)
			else
				task.defer(function()
					self:dungeonReset()
				end)
			end
		end,
		onenter_reset_dungeon = function(self)
			if not running then
				return
			end
			resetDungeon()
			self:fullReset()
		end,
	},
})

-- =========================================================================================================
-- Struct.
-- =========================================================================================================
local function buildStruct()
	return AutomationStruct.construct({
		id = "auto_saramed",
		state_machine = stateMachine,
		is_running = isRunning,
		features = {
			"no_fall",
			"noclip",
			"fly",
			"no_fire",
			"no_stun",
			"fast_swing",
			"m1_hold",
			"auto_equip_weapon",
			"auto_log",
		},
	})
end

-- Force Auto Defense (the parry/block engine) on for the run, remembering its previous state so stopping
-- the farm puts it back to whatever it was. Auto Saramed fights the dungeon mobs, so it wants defense up.
local function applyAutoDefense()
	if not Toggles or not Toggles.EnableAutoDefense then
		return
	end
	previousAutoDefense = Toggles.EnableAutoDefense.Value
	Toggles.EnableAutoDefense:SetValue(true)
end

local function restoreAutoDefense()
	if previousAutoDefense ~= nil and Toggles and Toggles.EnableAutoDefense then
		Toggles.EnableAutoDefense:SetValue(previousAutoDefense)
	end
	previousAutoDefense = nil
end

-- =========================================================================================================
-- Public interface.
-- =========================================================================================================
function AutoSaramed.start()
	local lp = localPlayer()
	if not lp then
		return
	end
	if running then
		return
	end
	if not AutoSaramed.inValidPlace() then
		return Logger.notify("Auto Saramed: not in the Eastern Luminant or the Saramed dungeon.")
	end
	local slot = currentSlot()
	if not slot then
		return Logger.notify("Auto Saramed: no data slot found.")
	end

	local data = PersistentData.get("sarameddata") or {}
	data.slot = slot
	data.startedAt = data.startedAt or os.time()
	PersistentData.set("sarameddata", data)

	running = true
	applyAutoDefense()
	buildOverlay()
	AntiAFK.start("AutoSaramed")

	maid:mark(task.spawn(runElapsed))

	stateMachine.current = "idle"
	stateMachine.asyncState = StateMachine.NONE
	stateMachine.currentTransitioningEvent = nil

	struct = buildStruct()
	struct:run()
end

function AutoSaramed.stop()
	if not running then
		return
	end
	running = false
	restoreAutoDefense()
	if struct then
		pcall(function()
			struct:stop()
		end)
		struct = nil
	end
	Tweening.stop(MOVE_ID)
	pcall(function()
		AntiAFK.stop("AutoSaramed")
	end)
	maid:clean()
	autoDepositThread = nil
	disconnectHandlerSet = false
	destroyOverlay()
	PersistentData.set("sarameddata", nil)
end

return AutoSaramed
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Automation/Objects/Teleports", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- Teleports: luma port of project-rain-oss's `generic_teleport` + the region teleport buttons
-- (etrean / eastern / depths). Each teleport forces NoClip, streams the destination in, then repeatedly
-- snaps to the destination and fires its RealmTeleport TouchInterest until the place change happens.
local Teleports = {}

local players = game:GetService("Players")
local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

local function localPlayer()
	return players.LocalPlayer
end

local function root()
	local lp = localPlayer()
	local char = lp and lp.Character
	return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function humanoid()
	local lp = localPlayer()
	local char = lp and lp.Character
	return char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Generic teleport (port of generic_teleport.lua).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
local GenericTeleport = {}
GenericTeleport.__index = GenericTeleport

function GenericTeleport.new(destination, destinationPartGetter, stopCondition)
	return setmetatable({
		lastStreamRequest = 0,
		destination = destination,
		destinationPartGetter = destinationPartGetter,
		stopCondition = stopCondition,
		conn = nil,
	}, GenericTeleport)
end

function GenericTeleport:requestStreamAroundDestination()
	if not workspace.StreamingEnabled then
		return
	end
	if tick() - self.lastStreamRequest > 1 / 30 then
		self.lastStreamRequest = tick()
		pcall(function()
			localPlayer():RequestStreamAroundAsync(self.destination.Position)
		end)
	end
end

function GenericTeleport:run()
	-- Force NoClip on for the trip (restore it if we turned it on).
	local setForUser = false
	if Toggles and Toggles.NoClip and not Toggles.NoClip.Value then
		setForUser = true
		Toggles.NoClip:SetValue(true)
	end

	self.conn = runService.PostSimulation:Connect(function()
		self:requestStreamAroundDestination()

		if self.stopCondition and self:stopCondition() then
			if self.conn then
				self.conn:Disconnect()
				self.conn = nil
			end
			if setForUser and Toggles and Toggles.NoClip then
				Toggles.NoClip:SetValue(false)
			end
			return
		end

		local r = root()
		local destinationPart = self.destinationPartGetter()
		if r and destinationPart and destinationPart:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest") then
			r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.one * (math.random() / 100)
			r.CFrame = self.destination * CFrame.new(math.random() / 100, math.random() / 100, math.random() / 100)
			xpcall(function()
				firetouchinterest(r, destinationPart, true)
				firetouchinterest(r, destinationPart, false)
			end, function() end)
		end

		local hum = humanoid()
		if hum and sethiddenproperty then
			pcall(function()
				sethiddenproperty(hum, "MoveDirectionInternal", Vector3.one)
			end)
		end
	end)

	return self
end

function GenericTeleport:stop()
	if self.conn then
		self.conn:Disconnect()
		self.conn = nil
	end
end

Teleports.GenericTeleport = GenericTeleport

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Region teleports. Each returns the GenericTeleport instance (so callers can :stop() it if needed).
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
local function valleyRealmTeleport()
	local exit = workspace:FindFirstChild("ValleyExit")
	return exit and exit:FindFirstChild("RealmTeleport")
end

---Etrean Luminant.
function Teleports.etrean()
	return GenericTeleport.new(CFrame.new(-528.434814, 704.674194, -4760.4292), valleyRealmTeleport):run()
end

---Eastern Luminant.
function Teleports.eastern()
	return GenericTeleport.new(
		CFrame.new(-2637.4563, 721.632935, -6722.51953, -0.757481456, 0, -0.652856648, 0, 1, 0, 0.652856648, 0, -0.757481456),
		valleyRealmTeleport
	):run()
end

---The Depths (via the Mod Office pit).
function Teleports.depths()
	return GenericTeleport.new(CFrame.new(39911.3672, 39980.9375, 39708.3203), function()
		local modOffice = workspace:FindFirstChild("ModOffice")
		return modOffice and modOffice:FindFirstChild("OfficePit")
	end):run()
end

return Teleports
end)
__bundle_register("Features/Automation/Objects/General", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
-- General: luma port of project-rain-oss's `general_utilitys` helpers used by the automation tasks.
-- The notable one is playing_ap_anims -- "is this entity currently in an attack-pattern animation" -- which
-- PR backs with its own 4.5k-line timing DB. Here it's backed by luma's OWN timing database
-- (SaveManager.as:index), so there's no duplicated data and it tracks luma's timings.
local General = {}

---@module Game.Timings.SaveManager
local SaveManager = require("Game/Timings/SaveManager")

local players = game:GetService("Players")
local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local FAST_SWING_CLASSES = { "OffhandAttack", "HeavyAttack", "MediumAttack", "LightAttack", "UsingSpell" }

---Tap a key once via VirtualInputManager (pure client input; no remote).
---@param keyCode Enum.KeyCode
function General.pressKey(keyCode)
	pcall(function()
		local vim = Instance.new("VirtualInputManager")
		vim:SendKeyEvent(true, keyCode, false, game)
		vim:SendKeyEvent(false, keyCode, false, game)
	end)
end

---Spam the A key to click through the game's loading screen (LoadingGui) until it disappears or the
---character spawns (10s cap). Input-only: the old Requests.StartMenu.Start remote is an anticheat
---honeypot (instant ban), so we drive the menu the way a player would instead of firing anything.
function General.clickThroughLoadingGui()
	-- Poll (up to 10s) for the loading screen OR the character being in -- not WaitForChild, so a character
	-- that loads in mid-wait bails immediately instead of stalling the full timeout.
	local loadingGui
	local findDeadline = os.clock() + 10
	repeat
		if players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer.Character then
			return
		end
		local pg = players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
		loadingGui = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("LoadingGui")
		if loadingGui then
			break
		end
		task.wait()
	until os.clock() >= findDeadline
	if not loadingGui then
		return
	end
	local deadline = os.clock() + 10
	repeat
		General.pressKey(Enum.KeyCode.A)
		task.wait(0.2)
	until not loadingGui.Parent or (players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer.Character) or os.clock() >= deadline
end

---Blow through the loading screen (was: fire Requests.StartMenu.Start). Now spams A through the
---LoadingGui instead -- the StartMenu remote is an AC honeypot. Kept under the same name so callers work.
function General.fireStartMenu()
	General.clickThroughLoadingGui()
end

---Fast swing: remove the attack-cooldown effects (LightAttack/HeavyAttack/etc.) the instant they're added,
---so swings aren't rate-limited client-side (PR's fast_swing). Returns the connection to disconnect, or nil.
function General.connectFastSwing()
	local er
	pcall(function()
		er = require(replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("EffectReplicator", 5))
	end)
	if not er or not er.EffectAdded then
		return nil
	end
	return er.EffectAdded:Connect(function(effect)
		if effect and effect.Class and table.find(FAST_SWING_CLASSES, effect.Class) then
			task.defer(function()
				pcall(function()
					effect:Remove(true)
				end)
			end)
		end
	end)
end

---Blow through the game's loading screen and wait for our character to spawn into workspace.Live.
---While the LoadingGui is up, spams A to click through it (input-only -- the old StartMenu.Start
---remote is an anticheat honeypot). Skipped for character creation (that's its own flow).
---@param isRunning function? returns false to bail early
function General.requestStart(isRunning)
	-- Already in-game (char in Live) or in the character creator? nothing to click through.
	local function inGame()
		local char = players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer.Character
		local creator = workspace:FindFirstChild("CharacterCreator")
		return (creator and char and char.Parent == creator)
			or (char and char.Parent and char.Parent.Name == "Live") and true
			or false
	end

	-- Wait (up to 10s) for EITHER the loading screen to appear OR the character to already be in. This is a
	-- poll, not WaitForChild, on purpose: WaitForChild blocks, so if the character loads in mid-wait it would
	-- stall the full timeout before returning (that was the "stalls a few seconds after the character loads"
	-- bug). Polling lets inGame() bail us out the instant the character is in. Called very early, so it also
	-- resolves PlayerGui here.
	local loadingGui
	local findDeadline = os.clock() + 10
	repeat
		if isRunning and not isRunning() then
			return
		end
		if inGame() then
			return
		end
		local pg = players.LocalPlayer and players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
		loadingGui = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("LoadingGui")
		if loadingGui then
			break
		end
		task.wait()
	until os.clock() >= findDeadline

	local deadline = os.clock() + 25
	repeat
		if isRunning and not isRunning() then
			return
		end
		if inGame() then
			return
		end
		-- Loading screen still up? spam A to click through it.
		if loadingGui and loadingGui.Parent then
			General.pressKey(Enum.KeyCode.A)
		end
		task.wait(0.2)
	until os.clock() >= deadline
end

---True if `entity` is currently playing any animation luma recognises as an attack timing. `ids` is an
---optional list of AnimationIds to ignore (PR passes specific ids it wants to treat as "safe").
---@param entity Instance
---@param ids table? list of AnimationId strings to skip
---@return boolean
function General.playing_ap_anims(entity, ids)
	if not entity then
		return false
	end
	local humanoid = entity:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if not humanoid then
		return false
	end
	local as = SaveManager and SaveManager.as
	if not as then
		return false
	end

	local ok, playing = pcall(function()
		return humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
	end)
	if not ok or not playing then
		return false
	end

	for _, track in next, playing do
		local full = tostring(track.Animation.AnimationId)
		if not (ids and table.find(ids, full)) then
			local digits = full:match("%d+")
			local matched = false
			pcall(function()
				matched = as:index(full) ~= nil or (digits ~= nil and as:index(digits) ~= nil)
			end)
			if matched then
				return true
			end
		end
	end
	return false
end

---Respawn / leave via the fastest route: replicate the Kill signal, then void ourselves if we somehow
---survived. Direct port of PR's `buttons/respawn`.
function General.respawn()
	local lp = players.LocalPlayer
	if not lp then
		return
	end
	if replicatesignal then
		pcall(function()
			replicatesignal(lp.Kill)
		end)
		task.delay(1, function()
			local char = lp.Character
			local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
			if hum and hum.Health <= 1 then
				return
			end
			local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if root then
				root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 9e9, 0)
			end
		end)
	else
		local char = lp.Character
		local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if root then
			root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 9e9, 0)
		end
	end
end

return General
end)
__bundle_register("Game/Timings/SaveManager", function(require, _LOADED, __bundle_register, __bundle_modules)
---@module Game.Timings.TimingSave
local TimingSave = require("Game/Timings/TimingSave")

---@module Game.Timings.TimingContainerPair
local TimingContainerPair = require("Game/Timings/TimingContainerPair")

---@module Game.Timings.TimingContainer
local TimingContainer = require("Game/Timings/TimingContainer")

---@module Game.Timings.AnimationTiming
local AnimationTiming = require("Game/Timings/AnimationTiming")

---@module Game.Timings.EffectTiming
local EffectTiming = require("Game/Timings/EffectTiming")

---@module Game.Timings.PartTiming
local PartTiming = require("Game/Timings/PartTiming")

---@module Game.Timings.SoundTiming
local SoundTiming = require("Game/Timings/SoundTiming")

---@module Utility.Maid
local Maid = require("Utility/Maid")

---@module Utility.Signal
local Signal = require("Utility/Signal")

---@module Utility.Configuration
local Configuration = require("Utility/Configuration")

local SaveManager = { llc = nil, llcn = nil, lct = nil, dirtyAt = nil }

---@note: How long after the last builder edit (seconds) we flush changes to disk. Keeps
---edit-to-disk latency low so a freshly-set value (e.g. an action hitbox) isn't lost if the
---user reloads before the long periodic interval elapses, while still debouncing slider drags.
local SAVE_DEBOUNCE = 1.0

---@module Utility.Filesystem
local Filesystem = require("Utility/Filesystem")

---@module Utility.Logger
local Logger = require("Utility/Logger")

---@module Utility.Deserializer
local Deserializer = require("Utility/Deserializer")

---@module Utility.String
local String = require("Utility/String")

---@module Utility.Serializer
local Serializer = require("Utility/Serializer")

local fs = Filesystem.new("Luma-Rewrite-Timings")

local config = TimingSave.new()

local runService = game:GetService("RunService")

local saveMaid = Maid.new()

---@return table
function SaveManager.list()
	local list = fs:list(true)
	local out = {}

	for idx = 1, #list do
		local file = list[idx]

		if file:sub(-4) ~= ".txt" then
			continue
		end

		local pos = file:find(".txt", 1, true)
		local char = file:sub(pos, pos)
		local start = pos

		while char ~= "/" and char ~= "\\" and char ~= "" do
			pos = pos - 1
			char = file:sub(pos, pos)
		end

		if char == "/" or char == "\\" then
			table.insert(out, file:sub(pos + 1, start - 1))
		end
	end

	return out
end

---@param dropdown table
function SaveManager.refresh(dropdown)
	dropdown:SetValues(SaveManager.list())
end

---@param name string
function SaveManager.autoload(name)
	if not name or #name <= 0 then
		return Logger.longNotify("Config name cannot be empty.")
	end

	local success, result = pcall(fs.write, fs, "autoload.txt", name)

	if not success then
		Logger.longNotify("Failed to write autoload file %s.", name)

		return Logger.warn(
			"Timing manager ran into the error '%s' while attempting to write autoload file %s.",
			result,
			name
		)
	end

	Logger.notify("Config file %s has set to auto-load.", name)
end

---@param name string
function SaveManager.create(name)
	if not name or #name <= 0 then
		return Logger.longNotify("Config name cannot be empty.")
	end

	if fs:file(name .. ".txt") then
		return Logger.longNotify("Config file %s already exists.", name)
	end

	SaveManager.write(name)
end

---@param name string
function SaveManager.save(name)
	if not name or #name <= 0 then
		return Logger.longNotify("Config name cannot be empty.")
	end

	if not fs:file(name .. ".txt") then
		return Logger.longNotify("Config file %s does not exist.", name)
	end

	SaveManager.write(name)
end

---@param name string
---@return number
function SaveManager.write(name, silent)
	if not name or #name <= 0 then
		return -1, Logger.longNotify("Config name cannot be empty.")
	end

	local success, result = pcall(Serializer.marshal, config:serialize())

	if not success then
		Logger.longNotify("Failed to serialize config file %s.", name)

		return -2,
			Logger.warn("Timing manager ran into the error '%s' while attempting to serialize config %s.", result, name)
	end

	success, result = pcall(fs.write, fs, name .. ".txt", result)

	if not success then
		Logger.longNotify("Failed to write config file %s.", name)

		return -3,
			Logger.warn("Timing manager ran into the error '%s' while attempting to write config %s.", result, name)
	end

	if not silent then
		Logger.notify("Config file %s has written to.", name)
	end

	return 0
end

---@param name string
function SaveManager.clear(name)
	if not name or #name <= 0 then
		return Logger.longNotify("Config name cannot be empty.")
	end

	local success, result = pcall(Serializer.marshal, TimingSave.new():serialize())

	if not success then
		Logger.longNotify("Failed to serialize config file %s.", name)

		return Logger.warn(
			"Timing manager ran into the error '%s' while attempting to serialize config %s.",
			result,
			name
		)
	end

	success, result = pcall(fs.write, fs, name .. ".txt", result)

	if not success then
		Logger.longNotify("Failed to write config file %s.", name)

		return Logger.warn("Timing manager ran into the error '%s' while attempting to write config %s.", result, name)
	end

	Logger.notify("Config file %s has been cleared.", name)
end

---@param name string
---@param silent boolean? Suppress the "config loaded" notification (used for startup autoload).
function SaveManager.load(name, silent)
	local timestamp = os.clock()

	if not name or #name <= 0 then
		return Logger.longNotify("Config name cannot be empty.")
	end

	local success, result = pcall(fs.read, fs, name .. ".txt")

	if not success then
		Logger.longNotify("Failed to read config file %s.", name)

		return Logger.warn("Timing manager ran into the error '%s' while attempting to read config %s.", result, name)
	end

	success, result = pcall(Deserializer.unmarshal_one, String.tba(result))

	if not success then
		Logger.longNotify("Failed to deserialize config file %s.", name)

		return Logger.warn(
			"Timing manager ran into the error '%s' while attempting to deserialize config %s.",
			result,
			name
		)
	end

	if typeof(result) ~= "table" then
		Logger.longNotify("Failed to process config file %s.", name)

		return Logger.warn("Timing manager failed to process config %s with result %s.", name, tostring(result))
	end

	config:clear()

	success, result = pcall(config.load, config, result)

	if not success then
		Logger.longNotify("Failed to load config file %s.", name)

		return Logger.warn("Timing manager ran into the error '%s' while attempting to load config %s.", result, name)
	end

	if not silent then
		Logger.notify(
			"Config file %s has loaded with %i timings in %.2f seconds.",
			name,
			config:count(),
			os.clock() - timestamp
		)
	end

	SaveManager.llc = config:clone()
	SaveManager.llcn = name
	SaveManager.dirtyAt = nil
end

---@note: Called by the timing builder whenever it mutates a timing/action (add an action, change
---a hitbox, etc.). Schedules a debounced flush so recent edits persist within ~1s instead of
---waiting for the periodic interval. No-op until a config is loaded.
function SaveManager.markDirty()
	if SaveManager.llcn then
		SaveManager.dirtyAt = os.clock()
	end
end

---@return boolean, number
function SaveManager.autosave()
	if not SaveManager.llcn then
		return false, -1, Logger.warn("No config name has been loaded for auto-save.")
	end

	if not SaveManager.sautos then
		return false, -2, Logger.warn("Auto-save has been disabled.")
	end

	Logger.warn("Auto-saving timings to '%s' config file.", SaveManager.llcn)

	return true, SaveManager.write(SaveManager.llcn), Logger.notify("Auto-save has completed successfully.")
end
